M4-11 Flaky budgeted-prewarm test (test_budgeted_prewarm_equals_batch[1e-05]) Done 11-13 20:15 ≈ 11-13 20:55 ≈ 40 min 0:00:00 0 0 Root cause: the test, not the decoder. The failing assert was `assert seen` (on_prewarm steps ran). With a 1e-5 s budget measured from the start of push_frames, beam.push alone usually uses the budget, so on_prewarm steps run only in rare pushes under 10 us (no load: 2 of ~130 prewarms per stream finished, seen = 10; under load seen = 0 in 1 of 3 runs). Decisions never depend on timing: the budget only decides how much of a pure memo (alignment pairs, LM rows, word caches) is filled; finalize recomputes any missing entry with the same function. Checked with an adversarial fake clock: 30 random schedules x 4 streams equal batch. Fix: injectable `CTCBeamSpec.clock` (default time.perf_counter) and `IncrementalPrefixBeam.prewarm(clock=...)`; the real-clock test now asserts results only; new deterministic tests test_budgeted_prewarm_fake_clock (schedules random / bursty / expired / frozen x 4 seeds; equality + coverage asserts) and test_beam_prewarm_fake_clock_cuts_mid_loop (deadline cut after 0..11 alignments). 50/50 runs of the budget tests under 8 busy loops (background class); full suite 650 passed / 9 skipped. Runs: M4-11 flaky prewarm test + P12-06 dataset survey + diagram 15 check

P5-22 Licence gate for input-event exporters Done 11-18 10:15 ≈ 11-18 10:50 ≈ 35 min 0:35:15 142,646 71 Open gap 61 (registered 2026-11-18 by P5-18). Follow-up of P5-18. DONE 2026-11-18: decision written (FEP-0010 decision 6, FEP-0006 section 4 rule 6): yes, an input-event exporter can refuse a research-only stream, per stream by its header keys. On a jsonl/stdout exporter whose types has an input event type, refuse_research_only judges each stream by its header (fidetype.trust.licence.stream_licence of meta, the same flags a trust layer copies into input.research_only) and drops a refused stream whole (header and every event of the same stream key up to its end). No key, or an event before any header: unknown licence, the P5-18 unknown_licence rule (refuse by default, pass exports). data.research_only = false without data.licence is research only (FEP-0010), also with pass. A new header on the same key starts a new verdict; end forgets the stream. Counters research_only_streams_refused, research_only_events_refused, licence_unknown_streams_refused, licence_unknown_events_refused; one log line per kind (no text, no session id). Items never changed (rule 1). A refused stream writes no file: per-session {session} files open at the first item; a single-path jsonl with the gate opens at its first exported item. Decisions-only exporters let input events pass (no stream counters). P5-18 config error (option on an input-event exporter) removed.
